([CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops](https://github.com/CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops/tree/HEAD/skills-library/skills/skill-watch) · ★1)
- **skill-watch**: Use when the user asks to monitor, improve, rewrite, debug, or evaluate agent skills, Claude Code hooks, Codex hooks, skill drift, missed skill activation, recurring workflow mistakes, or standards enforcement across dev/review/design/build skills. Also active through hooks to record expected skills, validation gaps, and recurring deviations.
